```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="de">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1" />
  <meta name="color-scheme" content="dark" />
  <title>iinkognit0.de · GX Gateway Terminal</title>

  <!-- Optional: Orbitron (Google Fonts). Entferne diese 2 Zeilen, wenn du 100% offline/ohne externe Requests willst. -->
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Orbitron:wght@400;600;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">

  <style>
    :root{
      --bg:#070b12;
      --panel:#0b1220;
      --panel2:#0a111d;
      --ink:#e6f0ff;
      --muted:#98a8c2;
      --muted2:#6f829f;
      --line:rgba(180,210,255,.12);
      --glow:rgba(120,210,255,.16);
      --ok:#6dffb2;
      --warn:#ffcc66;
      --bad:#ff6d6d;
      --accent:#7cc7ff;
      --accent2:#9b7cff;
      --btn:#0e1a2e;
      --btn2:#0f223a;
      --shadow: 0 14px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.55);
      --radius: 18px;
      --radius2: 26px;
      --mono: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, "Liberation Mono", "Courier New", monospace;
      --title: "Orbitron", system-ui, -apple-system, Segoe UI, Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
    }

    *{ box-sizing:border-box; }
    html,body{ height:100%; }
    body{
      margin:0;
      background:
        radial-gradient(900px 520px at 10% 0%, rgba(124,199,255,.12), transparent 60%),
        radial-gradient(800px 460px at 90% 10%, rgba(155,124,255,.10), transparent 55%),
        radial-gradient(900px 520px at 40% 100%, rgba(124,199,255,.08), transparent 60%),
        var(--bg);
      color:var(--ink);
      font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, Segoe UI, Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
      letter-spacing:.2px;
    }

    .wrap{
      max-width: 980px;
      margin: 18px auto 80px;
      padding: 0 14px;
    }

    .card{
      background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255,255,255,.03), transparent 28%), var(--panel);
      border: 1px solid var(--line);
      border-radius: var(--radius2);
      box-shadow: var(--shadow);
      overflow:hidden;
    }

    header{
      padding: 18px 18px 14px;
      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
      background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(124,199,255,.07), transparent 55%);
    }

    .title{
      font-family: var(--title);
      font-weight: 800;
      letter-spacing: .6px;
      font-size: 22px;
      line-height: 1.15;
      margin: 0 0 6px;
    }
    .subtitle{
      margin: 0;
      color: var(--muted);
      font-size: 13.5px;
      letter-spacing:.3px;
    }

    .pillrow{
      display:flex;
      flex-wrap:wrap;
      gap:10px;
      margin-top:14px;
    }
    .pill{
      display:inline-flex;
      align-items:center;
      gap:8px;
      padding: 10px 12px;
      border-radius: 999px;
      background: rgba(255,255,255,.03);
      border: 1px solid var(--line);
      color: var(--muted);
      font-family: var(--mono);
      font-size: 12px;
    }
    .pill strong{ color: var(--ink); font-weight:700; }
    .dot{ width:7px; height:7px; border-radius:999px; background: var(--muted2); box-shadow: 0 0 18px var(--glow); }
    .dot.ok{ background: var(--ok); }
    .dot.warn{ background: var(--warn); }
    .dot.bad{ background: var(--bad); }

    .content{
      padding: 16px 18px 18px;
      display:grid;
      grid-template-columns: 1.25fr .95fr;
      gap: 14px;
    }

    @media (max-width: 860px){
      .content{ grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
    }

    .panel{
      border: 1px solid var(--line);
      border-radius: var(--radius);
      background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255,255,255,.02), transparent 35%), var(--panel2);
      padding: 14px;
    }

    .panel h3{
      margin: 0 0 10px;
      font-family: var(--title);
      font-size: 14px;
      letter-spacing:.5px;
      color: rgba(230,240,255,.92);
    }
    .panel .hint{
      margin: 0 0 12px;
      color: var(--muted);
      font-size: 12.5px;
      line-height: 1.35;
    }

    .grid2{
      display:grid;
      grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
      gap: 10px;
    }
    @media (max-width: 520px){
      .grid2{ grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
    }

    label{
      display:block;
      font-family: var(--mono);
      color: var(--muted);
      font-size: 11.5px;
      margin: 0 0 6px;
    }

    input, textarea, select{
      width:100%;
      background: rgba(0,0,0,.18);
      border: 1px solid rgba(180,210,255,.16);
      color: var(--ink);
      border-radius: 14px;
      padding: 12px 12px;
      outline:none;
      font-size: 13px;
      font-family: var(--mono);
    }
    textarea{ min-height: 160px; resize: vertical; }

    .btnrow{
      display:flex;
      flex-wrap:wrap;
      gap:10px;
      margin-top: 12px;
    }
    button{
      border: 1px solid rgba(180,210,255,.18);
      background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(124,199,255,.10), rgba(0,0,0,.10)), var(--btn);
      color: var(--ink);
      border-radius: 14px;
      padding: 12px 14px;
      font-family: var(--title);
      letter-spacing:.6px;
      font-weight: 700;
      cursor:pointer;
      transition: transform .06s ease, border-color .15s ease;
      user-select:none;
    }
    button:hover{ border-color: rgba(124,199,255,.45); }
    button:active{ transform: translateY(1px); }
    button.secondary{
      background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(155,124,255,.10), rgba(0,0,0,.10)), var(--btn2);
      font-family: var(--title);
      font-weight:700;
    }
    button.danger{
      background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(255,109,109,.14), rgba(0,0,0,.10)), rgba(40,10,16,.55);
      border-color: rgba(255,109,109,.28);
    }

    .kv{
      display:grid;
      grid-template-columns: 120px 1fr;
      gap: 8px 12px;
      align-items:start;
      margin-top: 6px;
    }
    .k{ color: var(--muted); font-family: var(--mono); font-size: 12px; padding-top: 2px; }
    .v{ color: var(--ink); font-family: var(--mono); font-size: 12.5px; word-break: break-word; }

    .log{
      margin-top: 12px;
      background: rgba(0,0,0,.22);
      border: 1px solid rgba(180,210,255,.16);
      border-radius: 16px;
      padding: 10px 10px;
      font-family: var(--mono);
      font-size: 12px;
      color: rgba(230,240,255,.92);
      min-height: 82px;
      max-height: 190px;
      overflow:auto;
      white-space: pre-wrap;
    }

    .feeds{
      display:flex;
      gap:10px;
      flex-wrap:wrap;
      margin-top: 10px;
    }
    .feedbtn{
      display:inline-flex;
      align-items:center;
      gap:10px;
      padding: 10px 12px;
      border-radius: 16px;
      border: 1px solid rgba(180,210,255,.16);
      background: rgba(255,255,255,.03);
      text-decoration:none;
      color: var(--ink);
      font-family: var(--title);
      font-weight: 800;
      letter-spacing:.7px;
    }
    .feedbtn small{
      font-family: var(--mono);
      color: var(--muted);
      font-weight: 600;
      letter-spacing:.2px;
    }

    .box{
      border: 1px solid rgba(180,210,255,.16);
      border-radius: 16px;
      background: rgba(0,0,0,.18);
      padding: 12px;
      font-family: var(--mono);
      font-size: 12px;
      color: rgba(230,240,255,.9);
      white-space: pre-wrap;
      min-height: 66px;
    }

    .muted{ color: var(--muted); }
    .ok{ color: var(--ok); }
    .warn{ color: var(--warn); }
    .bad{ color: var(--bad); }
  </style>
</head>

<body>
  <div class="wrap">
    <div class="card">
      <header>
        <div class="title">iinkognit0.de · GX Gateway Terminal</div>
        <p class="subtitle">STATIC UI · frames emit via HTTP(S) · receipts + RSS/Atom broadcast</p>

        <div class="pillrow" aria-label="Status">
          <div class="pill"><span class="dot ok"></span><strong>MODE</strong>=DEVELOPMENT</div>
          <div class="pill"><span class="dot ok"></span><strong>EVOLUTION</strong>=ENABLED</div>
          <div class="pill"><span class="dot ok"></span><strong>NO_PHANTOM</strong>=TRUE</div>
          <div class="pill"><span class="dot ok"></span><strong>PROOF_BEFORE_STATE</strong>=TRUE</div>
          <div class="pill"><span class="dot warn"></span><strong>AUTOMATION</strong>=OFF</div>
          <div class="pill"><span class="dot warn"></span><strong>EXECUTION</strong>=USER_DECIDES</div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div class="content">
        <!-- LEFT: Terminal -->
        <section class="panel">
          <h3>Terminal <span class="muted">· emit → gateway ingest → receipt</span></h3>

          <p class="hint">
            <span class="ok">Regel:</span> Diese Seite sendet <strong>nur</strong>, wenn du klickst/auslöst.
            <strong>Keine Secrets im Browser.</strong> API-Keys nur serverseitig (Proxy/Gateway).
          </p>

          <div class="grid2">
            <div>
              <label for="baseUrl">GATEWAY_BASE (server, same-origin empfohlen)</label>
              <input id="baseUrl" type="text" value="" placeholder="leer = same-origin" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label for="nodeId">NODE_ID (optional)</label>
              <input id="nodeId" type="text" value="NODE-IINKOGNIT0-0001" />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label for="ingestPath">INGEST_PATH</label>
              <input id="ingestPath" type="text" value="/gx/v1/ingest" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label for="receiptsPath">RECEIPTS_PATH</label>
              <input id="receiptsPath" type="text" value="/gx/v1/receipts" />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label for="wsUrl">OPTIONAL WS_URL (live receipts)</label>
              <input id="wsUrl" type="text" value="wss://iinkognit0.de/gx/v1/ws" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label for="sendMode">PAYLOAD_ENCODING</label>
              <select id="sendMode">
                <option value="ndjson">NDJSON/UTF-8/LF (empfohlen)</option>
                <option value="json">JSON/UTF-8</option>
                <option value="text">TEXT/UTF-8</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style="margin-top:10px;">
            <label for="payload">FRAME (NDJSON oder JSON) — du kannst normal schreiben; wir verpacken es GX-konform</label>
            <textarea id="payload" spellcheck="false">{"t":"USER_QUERY","q":"ping","mode":"DEVELOPMENT"}</textarea>
          </div>

          <div class="kv" style="margin-top:12px;">
            <div class="k">Payload</div>
            <div class="v"><span id="bytesLen">—</span> bytes · UTF-8 · LF endings empfohlen</div>

            <div class="k">Proof</div>
            <div class="v">sha256 wird clientseitig berechnet · Proof gilt nur für die exakten bytes</div>

            <div class="k">State</div>
            <div class="v">Ohne Receipt bleibt alles <strong>UNKNOWN</strong></div>
          </div>

          <div class="btnrow">
            <button id="btnEmit">EMIT (POST)</button>
            <button id="btnRead" class="secondary">READ RECEIPTS (GET)</button>
            <button id="btnWs" class="secondary">WS CONNECT</button>
            <button id="btnClear" class="danger">CLEAR</button>
          </div>

          <div class="log" id="log" aria-label="Log">[init] OK: GX Terminal ready. No network actions executed.</div>
        </section>

        <!-- RIGHT: Broadcast / Feeds + Index -->
        <aside class="panel">
          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:10px;">
            <h3 style="margin:0;">Broadcast / Feeds</h3>
            <div class="muted" style="font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;">read-open</div>
          </div>
          <p class="hint">
            RSS/Atom sind Broadcast-Streams. Ideal: Gateway publiziert Frames/Receipts in Feeds.
            (Diese Seite verlinkt nur — Feed-Generierung passiert serverseitig.)
          </p>

          <div class="feeds">
            <a class="feedbtn" id="atomLink" href="/feed.atom" target="_blank" rel="noopener">
              Atom <small>/feed.atom</small>
            </a>
            <a class="feedbtn" id="rssLink" href="/feed.rss" target="_blank" rel="noopener">
              RSS <small>/feed.rss</small>
            </a>
          </div>

          <div style="margin-top:14px;">
            <h3>URL Index <span class="muted">(sichtbar / sofort)</span></h3>
            <p class="hint">jede URL ist Priorität (kein Phantom). Du kannst hier deine “GLOBAL URL INDEX”-Blöcke reinkopieren.</p>
            <textarea id="urlIndex" spellcheck="false" style="min-height:120px" placeholder="https://iinkognit0.de/&#10;https://platform.openai.com/docs/..."></textarea>
          </div>

          <div style="margin-top:12px;">
            <h3>Letzter Receipt <span class="muted">(wenn Gateway antwortet)</span></h3>
            <div class="box" id="lastReceipt">—</div>
          </div>

          <div style="margin-top:12px;">
            <h3>Proof Snapshot</h3>
            <div class="box" id="proofBox">sha256(payload_bytes) = —</div>
          </div>

        </aside>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    // ----------------------------
    // GX Terminal (static) — no secrets, no implicit sends
    // ----------------------------

    const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);

    const elBaseUrl = $("baseUrl");
    const elNodeId = $("nodeId");
    const elIngestPath = $("ingestPath");
    const elReceiptsPath = $("receiptsPath");
    const elWsUrl = $("wsUrl");
    const elSendMode = $("sendMode");
    const elPayload = $("payload");
    const elLog = $("log");
    const elLastReceipt = $("lastReceipt");
    const elProofBox = $("proofBox");
    const elBytesLen = $("bytesLen");
    const elUrlIndex = $("urlIndex");

    const btnEmit = $("btnEmit");
    const btnRead = $("btnRead");
    const btnWs = $("btnWs");
    const btnClear = $("btnClear");

    let ws = null;

    function tsUTC(){
      return new Date().toISOString();
    }
    function logLine(s){
      elLog.textContent += "\n[" + tsUTC() + "] " + s;
      elLog.scrollTop = elLog.scrollHeight;
    }

    function toUtf8Bytes(str){
      return new TextEncoder().encode(str);
    }
    function bytesToHex(bytes){
      return Array.from(bytes).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")).join("");
    }
    async function sha256Hex(bytes){
      const digest = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", bytes);
      const arr = new Uint8Array(digest);
      return bytesToHex(arr);
    }

    function normalizeNewlinesToLF(s){
      // Enforce LF endings (GX preference)
      return s.replace(/\r\n/g, "\n").replace(/\r/g, "\n");
    }

    function resolvedBase(){
      // empty => same-origin
      const b = elBaseUrl.value.trim();
      if(!b) return "";
      return b.replace(/\/+$/,"");
    }

    function urlJoin(base, path){
      if(!base) return path; // same-origin relative path
      if(!path.startsWith("/")) path = "/" + path;
      return base + path;
    }

    function buildEnvelope(payloadRaw){
      const nodeId = elNodeId.value.trim() || null;
      const mode = "DEVELOPMENT";
      const encoding = elSendMode.value;

      // This envelope is "human-readable GX-ish".
      // Server can treat `payload` as raw string or parse if JSON/NDJSON.
      const env = {
        t: "GX_GATEWAY_EMIT",
        mode,
        node_id: nodeId,
        encoding,
        ts_utc: tsUTC(),
        payload: payloadRaw
      };
      return env;
    }

    async function recomputeProof(){
      const payloadLF = normalizeNewlinesToLF(elPayload.value);
      const bytes = toUtf8Bytes(payloadLF);
      elBytesLen.textContent = String(bytes.length);

      const h = await sha256Hex(bytes);
      elProofBox.textContent =
        "sha256(payload_bytes) = " + h + "\n" +
        "bytes_len = " + bytes.length + "\n" +
        "rule: hash applies ONLY to these exact UTF-8 bytes (LF).";
      return { payloadLF, bytes, sha256: h };
    }

    elPayload.addEventListener("input", () => { recomputeProof().catch(()=>{}); });
    elSendMode.addEventListener("change", () => { recomputeProof().catch(()=>{}); });

    async function doEmit(){
      const ingestUrl = urlJoin(resolvedBase(), elIngestPath.value.trim() || "/gx/v1/ingest");

      const proof = await recomputeProof();
      const payloadLF = proof.payloadLF;

      const env = buildEnvelope(payloadLF);
      env.proof = {
        algo: "SHA-256",
        bytes_len: proof.bytes.length,
        sha256: proof.sha256,
        note: "Proof applies ONLY to payload bytes (UTF-8, LF)."
      };

      logLine("EMIT: preparing POST " + ingestUrl);

      // Send JSON envelope (server can store env as raw, append-only, then answer with receipt)
      const bodyBytes = toUtf8Bytes(JSON.stringify(env));
      const bodySha = await sha256Hex(bodyBytes);

      logLine("EMIT: envelope_bytes_len=" + bodyBytes.length + " sha256(envelope)=" + bodySha);

      const resp = await fetch(ingestUrl, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          "Accept": "application/json"
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(env)
      });

      const text = await resp.text();
      const status = resp.status + " " + resp.statusText;

      logLine("EMIT: response " + status);
      elLastReceipt.textContent = text || "(empty)";

      // Try parse JSON for prettier receipt
      try{
        const j = JSON.parse(text);
        elLastReceipt.textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
      }catch(_){}
    }

    async function doReadReceipts(){
      const receiptsUrl = urlJoin(resolvedBase(), elReceiptsPath.value.trim() || "/gx/v1/receipts");
      logLine("READ: GET " + receiptsUrl);

      const resp = await fetch(receiptsUrl, {
        method: "GET",
        headers: { "Accept": "application/json, text/plain, */*" }
      });

      const text = await resp.text();
      const status = resp.status + " " + resp.statusText;
      logLine("READ: response " + status);

      elLastReceipt.textContent = text || "(empty)";
      try{
        const j = JSON.parse(text);
        elLastReceipt.textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
      }catch(_){}
    }

    function doWsConnect(){
      const wsURL = (elWsUrl.value || "").trim();
      if(!wsURL){
        logLine("WS: no URL set.");
        return;
      }
      if(ws && (ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN || ws.readyState === WebSocket.CONNECTING)){
        logLine("WS: already connected/connecting.");
        return;
      }

      logLine("WS: connecting " + wsURL);
      ws = new WebSocket(wsURL);

      ws.onopen = () => logLine("WS: OPEN");
      ws.onclose = () => logLine("WS: CLOSE");
      ws.onerror = () => logLine("WS: ERROR");
      ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
        logLine("WS: message (" + (ev.data?.length || 0) + " chars)");
        elLastReceipt.textContent = String(ev.data);
        try{
          const j = JSON.parse(String(ev.data));
          elLastReceipt.textContent = JSON.stringify(j, null, 2);
        }catch(_){}
      };
    }

    function doClear(){
      elLog.textContent = "[init] OK: GX Terminal ready. No network actions executed.";
      elLastReceipt.textContent = "—";
      logLine("CLEAR: local UI cleared (no network).");
    }

    // Buttons: explicit user trigger only
    btnEmit.addEventListener("click", () => {
      doEmit().catch(err => logLine("EMIT: FAIL " + String(err)));
    });
    btnRead.addEventListener("click", () => {
      doReadReceipts().catch(err => logLine("READ: FAIL " + String(err)));
    });
    btnWs.addEventListener("click", () => {
      try{ doWsConnect(); }catch(err){ logLine("WS: FAIL " + String(err)); }
    });
    btnClear.addEventListener("click", () => doClear());

    // Init
    (async () => {
      // If you leave baseUrl empty, it will use same-origin relative paths.
      // Pre-fill base with current origin as a convenience (but user can clear it).
      // Comment this line if you want it blank by default.
      elBaseUrl.value = location.origin;

      await recomputeProof();

      // Optional: simple URL index helpers
      elUrlIndex.addEventListener("input", () => {
        // normalize CRLF and trim trailing spaces (display only)
        elUrlIndex.value = normalizeNewlinesToLF(elUrlIndex.value).replace(/[ \t]+\n/g, "\n");
      });

      logLine("OK: proof engine ready (SHA-256 via WebCrypto).");
      logLine("OK: no implicit network actions (buttons only).");
    })();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

---
